1455: +func TestInterfaceParsing(t *testing.T) {
1456: +	tests := map[string]struct {
1457: +		ifaces  []string
1458: +		want    []string
1459: +		wantErr bool
1460: +	}{
1461: +		"routeros-names": {
1462: +			ifaces: []string{"ether2", "ether5", "ether13"},
1463: +			want:   []string{"eth1", "eth4", "eth12"},
1464: +		},
1465: +		"eth-names":   {ifaces: []string{"eth1", "eth3"}, want: []string{"eth1", "eth3"}},
1466: +		"mgmt-ether1": {ifaces: []string{"ether1"}, wantErr: true},
1467: +	}
1468: +
1469: +	for name, tc := range tests {
1470: +		t.Run(name, func(t *testing.T) {
1471: +			n := newInitialized(t, &clabtypes.NodeConfig{ShortName: "r1", LabDir: t.TempDir()})
1472: +
1473: +			var err error
1474: +			for _, ifName := range tc.ifaces {
1475: +				ep := &clablinks.EndpointVeth{
1476: +					EndpointGeneric: clablinks.EndpointGeneric{IfaceName: ifName},
1477: +				}
1478: +				if err = n.AddEndpoint(ep); err != nil {
1479: +					break
1480: +				}
1481: +			}
1482: +			if err == nil {
1483: +				err = n.CheckInterfaceName()
1484: +			}
1485: +
1486: +			if tc.wantErr {
1487: +				if err == nil {
1488: +					t.Fatalf("interfaces %v: want an error, got none", tc.ifaces)
1489: +				}
1490: +				return
1491: +			}
1492: +			if err != nil {
1493: +				t.Fatalf("interfaces %v: %v", tc.ifaces, err)
1494: +			}
1495: +			for i, ep := range n.Endpoints {
1496: +				if ep.GetIfaceName() != tc.want[i] {
1497: +					t.Errorf(
1498: +						"%q mapped to %q, want %q",
1499: +						tc.ifaces[i],
1500: +						ep.GetIfaceName(),
1501: +						tc.want[i],
1502: +					)
1503: +				}
1504: +			}
1505: +		})
1506: +	}
1507: +}
1508: +
1509: +// exportedConfig is a verbatim RouterOS 7.24.5 /export of a node this kind deployed
1510: +// (transparent management, IPv4 + IPv6, a lab default route in main).
1511: +const exportedConfig = `# 2026-10-16 21:56:34 by RouterOS 7.24.5
1512: +# system id = db75dropC8O
1513: +#
1514: +/interface bridge
1515: +add name=lo0
1516: +/interface ethernet
1517: +set [ find default-name=ether1 ] disable-running-check=no
1518: +set [ find default-name=ether2 ] disable-running-check=no
1519: +/routing table
1520: +add comment=clab-mgmt fib name=mgmt
1521: +/ip address
1522: +add address=172.20.20.4/24 comment=clab-mgmt interface=ether1 network=\
1523: +    172.20.20.0
1524: +add address=10.0.0.2/30 interface=ether2 network=10.0.0.0
1525: +add address=2.2.2.2 interface=lo0 network=2.2.2.2
1526: +/ip dhcp-client
1527: +add disabled=yes interface=ether1 name=client1
1528: +/ip route
1529: +add comment=clab-mgmt dst-address=172.20.20.0/24 gateway=ether1 \
1530: +    routing-table=mgmt
1531: +add comment=clab-mgmt dst-address=0.0.0.0/0 gateway=172.20.20.1 \
1532: +    routing-table=mgmt
1533: +add dst-address=0.0.0.0/0 gateway=10.0.0.1
1534: +/ipv6 route
1535: +add comment=clab-mgmt dst-address=3fff:172:20:20::4/64 gateway=ether1 \
1536: +    routing-table=mgmt
1537: +add comment=clab-mgmt dst-address=::/0 gateway=3fff:172:20:20::1%ether1 \
1538: +    routing-table=mgmt
1539: +/ipv6 address
1540: +add address=3fff:172:20:20::4 advertise=no comment=clab-mgmt interface=ether1
1541: +/ipv6 nd
1542: +add interface=ether1 ra-lifetime=none
1543: +/routing rule
1544: +add action=lookup-only-in-table comment=clab-mgmt src-address=172.20.20.4/32 \
1545: +    table=mgmt
1546: +add action=lookup-only-in-table comment=clab-mgmt src-address=\
1547: +    3fff:172:20:20::4/128 table=mgmt
1548: +/system identity
1549: +set name=r2`

1551: +func TestFilterLauncherConfig(t *testing.T) {
1552: +	got := filterLauncherConfig(exportedConfig)
1553: +
1554: +	if strings.Contains(got, "clab-mgmt") {
1555: +		t.Errorf("filtered config still contains a clab-mgmt object:\n%s", got)
1556: +	}
1557: +	for _, gone := range []string{
1558: +		"add disabled=yes interface=ether1 name=client1",
1559: +		"add interface=ether1 ra-lifetime=none",
1560: +		"172.20.20.4",
1561: +		"3fff:172:20:20::4",
1562: +		"198.18",
1563: +	} {
1564: +		if strings.Contains(got, gone) {
1565: +			t.Errorf("filtered config still contains launcher line %q:\n%s", gone, got)
1566: +		}
1567: +	}
1568: +
1569: +	for _, kept := range []string{
1570: +		"add address=10.0.0.2/30 interface=ether2 network=10.0.0.0",
1571: +		"add address=2.2.2.2 interface=lo0 network=2.2.2.2",
1572: +		"add dst-address=0.0.0.0/0 gateway=10.0.0.1",
1573: +		"add name=lo0",
1574: +		"set name=r2",
1575: +		"/routing table",
1576: +	} {
1577: +		if !strings.Contains(got, kept) {
1578: +			t.Errorf("filtered config lost user line %q:\n%s", kept, got)
1579: +		}
1580: +	}
1581: +}
1582: +
1583: +func TestFilterLauncherConfigContinuationLines(t *testing.T) {
1584: +	in := "/ip route\n" +
1585: +		"add comment=clab-mgmt dst-address=0.0.0.0/0 \\\n" +
1586: +		"    gateway=198.18.255.2 routing-table=mgmt\n" +
1587: +		"add dst-address=10.9.0.0/16 gateway=10.0.0.2 \\\n" +
1588: +		"    routing-table=main\n"
1589: +
1590: +	got := filterLauncherConfig(in)
1591: +
1592: +	if strings.Contains(got, "198.18.255.2") {
1593: +		t.Errorf("wrapped launcher route kept:\n%s", got)
1594: +	}
1595: +	if !strings.Contains(
1596: +		got,
1597: +		"add dst-address=10.9.0.0/16 gateway=10.0.0.2 \\\n    routing-table=main",
1598: +	) {
1599: +		t.Errorf("wrapped user route lost or reflowed:\n%s", got)
1600: +	}
1601: +}
1602: +
1603: +func TestFilterLauncherConfigEscapedBackslash(t *testing.T) {
1604: +	// A value ending in a literal backslash is exported as `\\`: that line is complete and
1605: +	// must not swallow the launcher line after it.
1606: +	in := "/ip address\n" +
1607: +		"add address=10.9.9.1/24 interface=ether2 network=10.9.9.0 comment=path\\\\\n" +
1608: +		"add address=172.20.20.3/24 comment=clab-mgmt interface=ether1 network=\\\n" +
1609: +		"    172.20.20.0\n"
1610: +
1611: +	got := filterLauncherConfig(in)
1612: +
1613: +	if !strings.Contains(
1614: +		got,
1615: +		"add address=10.9.9.1/24 interface=ether2 network=10.9.9.0 comment=path",
1616: +	) {
1617: +		t.Errorf("user address ending in an escaped backslash was dropped:\n%s", got)
1618: +	}
1619: +	if strings.Contains(got, "clab-mgmt") {
1620: +		t.Errorf("launcher address kept:\n%s", got)
1621: +	}
1622: +}
1623: +
1624: +func TestContinues(t *testing.T) {
1625: +	for line, want := range map[string]bool{
1626: +		`add a=b`:     false,
1627: +		`add a=b \`:   true,
1628: +		`add a=b\\`:   false,
1629: +		`add a=b\\ \`: true,
1630: +		`add a=b\\\`:  true,
1631: +		`add a=b\\\\`: false,
1632: +	} {
1633: +		if got := continues(line); got != want {
1634: +			t.Errorf("continues(%q) = %v, want %v", line, got, want)
1635: +		}
1636: +	}
1637: +}

Need to check.

2. Password env var: `PASSWORD="${PASSWORD-admin}"` — note that's `${PASSWORD-admin}`, not `${PASSWORD:-admin}`. Difference: `-` uses default also when set-but-empty; `:-` uses default only when unset-or-empty. Actually `${PASSWORD-admin}` gives default when PASSWORD is unset OR null(empty)? In POSIX sh, `${var-word}` uses word if var is unset; `${var:-word}` uses word if unset or null. So `${PASSWORD-admin}` keeps an empty password as empty. That's deliberate: user can set empty password. Fine.

3. The healthcheck: `StartPeriod: 300` and `CHR_BOOT_WAIT` default 300 — start period equals boot wait; healthcheck begins before ready marker. Minor.

4. `resp_complete` and `rest_raw`: use fifo, background nc, etc. Look for bugs: `timeout "$(( $4 + 5 ))" nc ...` fine. `exec 7>"$_f"` — opens the fifo for writing, then prints request. busybox nc reading from fifo. The loop waits. Then `exec 7>&-` closes. Seems okay. But if resp_complete never completes and nc exits, `kill -0` fails and loop exits. OK.

Potential bug: `rest_raw` is only used with `rest_add` (20s) and `import` (BOOT_WAIT). Fine.

5. `rest_clear` removes objects matching comment. Note `rest_has "ip/route?routing-table=..."` etc.

6. `rest_count` counts `".id"` occurrences via `tr '{' '\n' | grep -c '"\.id"'` — grep -c counts lines; if grep returns no match, exit code 1, but command substitution just takes empty → 0? Actually `grep -c` prints "0" and exits 1. With `set -u` but no `set -e`, fine. But wait — `rest "$AUTH" "$1"` inside a pipeline... fine.

Hmm, but there is `set -u` only, no `set -e`. OK.

7. `count_intfs` uses `ls -d ... | wc -l` — if no matches, ls errors to /dev/null and wc -l gives 0. OK.

8. Disk growth: `stat -c %s` fallback `wc -c < "$IMG"`.

9. `MEM` calculation: 256 + 8*CPUS*max. If max=0 (no data NICs, INTFS=0), MEM=256. OK.

10. Wait — in the `while [ "$i" -le "$max" ]` loop, mac uses `$((i & 255))` — for i up to 255 fine; beyond that, wraps. Node with more than 255 interfaces? Unlikely.

11. `VM_LL="fe80::5054:ff:fe${node_id%%:*}:${node_id##*:}00"` — node_id is like "aa:bb". So VM_LL = fe80::5054:ff:feaa:bb00. The MAC is 52:54:00:$node_id:00 → 52:54:00:aa:bb:00. EUI-64 link-local from MAC 52:54:00:aa:bb:00: flip bit 7 of first octet: 52 = 0x52 = 0101 0010 → flip U/L bit → 0x50. So fe80::5054:ff:feaa:bb00. Correct.

Bridge MAC: `52:54:01:$node_id:00`. Note bridge LL address is generated by kernel from bridge MAC: 52:54:01 → fe80::5054:01ff:feaa:bb00. The `bridge_ll` function reads it dynamically. OK.

12. `ip_global` sed: `'/scope global/s/^ *inet6\{0,1\} \([^ ]*\) .*/\1/p'` — matches both inet and inet6 lines with scope global. For IPv4 address show: "inet 172.20.20.4/24 ... scope global eth0". OK.

13. G4/G6: `ip -4 route show default` — but this is run BEFORE the bridge is created and eth0 flushed? Let's check order: M4/G4 captured before `ip addr flush dev eth0`. Yes, lines: M4, M6, G4, G6 captured at line ~560 before bridge creation. Good.

But wait — on restart, eth0 may already be bridged and bare (no address). Then M4/M6 empty, and it reads from MGMT_FILE. Good — that's the point of MGMT_FILE.

14. watch_links: `_warned` accumulation. OK.

15. `trap 'kill ...' TERM INT` set after qemu starts. OK.

16. The `ready` function: `touch "$READY_FILE" 2>/dev/null || true`. OK.

17. `bootstrap_fail` — `wait "$QEMU_PID"` then exit 0. Hmm, exits 0 on failure — the container's main process exits 0, so container stops with code 0. Healthcheck will never mark ready. Node stays "exited"? Actually if the entrypoint exits, the container stops. Then the node is not running. Hmm — "A failed step WITHHOLDS the ready marker (qemu keeps running...)" — wait "$QEMU_PID" waits for qemu to exit, but qemu doesn't exit by itself! wait would block forever until TERM. So bootstrap_fail blocks indefinitely holding qemu. But the entrypoint is the launcher; if it exits, the container dies. They deliberately wait forever so the container keeps running. But then `docker stop` sends TERM to PID 1 which is the launcher (waiting in `wait`); trap kills qemu and exits. OK, deliberate design.

But note: on the r3 test, the import failed → wait $QEMU_PID blocks forever → container stays up, log contains failure, healthcheck never passes → "r3 never becomes healthy". OK.

However — during the failed import path, the test expects the node to remain... fine.

18. Security: password passed via env. Env vars visible via docker inspect — that's a known tradeoff, noted in comment.

19. `SaveConfig` uses `n.Cfg.LongName` host with port 22 — LongName for containerlab nodes is like "clab-lab-r1", which resolves via docker DNS. OK.

20. `filterLauncherConfig`: uses `strings.SplitSeq` (Go 1.24+). Check go.mod version. Also section tracking: `if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "/")` sets section. But RouterOS export sections like `/ip address` then `add ...`. launcherOwned matches `interface=ether1` in fields after replacing `\` with space. For continuation lines, joined with "\n" so `strings.Fields` splits on whitespace incl newline. OK.

Potential issue: `/ip dhcp-client` section: `add disabled=yes interface=ether1 name=client1` → dropped by interface=ether1 rule. But what if a user has a DHCP client on ether1 legitimately? It would be dropped from saved config. Minor; by design (launcher owns ether1).

But there's a subtle bug: the `section` variable — for `add comment=clab-mgmt` under any section it's dropped. For `/ip route` `add ... routing-table=mgmt` — tagged clab-mgmt → dropped. Fine.

What about lines like `set [ find default-name=ether1 ] ...`? Not "add ", kept. Good.

21. `continues()` handles backslashes.

22. The `exportedConfig` test expects "198.18" not present — but 198.18 doesn't appear in exportedConfig... The test checks `strings.Contains(got, "198.18")` where got is filtered exportedConfig. 198.18 never appears in exportedConfig — dead assertion (always passes). Minor test quality note.

28. CheckInterfaceName: InterfaceRegexp `ether(?P<port>\d+)`, Offset 2. So ether1 → port 1 → eth(1-1)=eth0? Wait, the test says ether1 → error, ether2 → eth1. So offset logic: eth index = port - InterfaceOffset? port 2 - 2 = 0 → eth0? But test wants eth1. Hmm. Let me check VRNode/VMNode CheckInterfaceName implementation to verify. Actually offset semantics vary. The test asserts ether2→eth1, ether5→eth4, ether13→eth12. So eth = port - 1. But InterfaceOffset = 2. Let me check the base implementation.

Also note: ethN mapping — container ethN is RouterOS ether(N+1), so ether2 = eth1 → port-1. If offset logic is `port - InterfaceOffset`, that gives port-2 = eth0 for ether2, contradiction. Unless the offset is used as `port - offset + 1` or the base code treats offset differently. Need to read the actual base implementation.

Also ether1 must error: since eth index would be 0 which collides with mgmt. Need to check the base validates ≥0 vs >0.

183: 	// subsequent starts when they try to rediscover a versioned qcow filename. If there is exactly
184: 	// one non-overlay qcow file in / and our alias is absent, create a hardlink alias based on the
185: 	// image tag.
186: 	cmd := fmt.Sprintf(
187: 		`alias="/%s"; `+
188: 			`[ -e "$alias" ] && exit 0; `+
189: 			`src=""; `+
190: 			`if [ -f /sros.qcow2 ] && [ "/sros.qcow2" != "$alias" ]; then `+
191: 			`src="/sros.qcow2"; `+
192: 			`else `+
193: 			`set -- /*.qcow2; `+
194: 			`if [ "$1" != "/*.qcow2" ]; then `+
195: 			`for f in "$@"; do `+
196: 			`[ "$f" = "$alias" ] && continue; `+
197: 			`base="${f##*/}"; `+
198: 			`case "$base" in *overlay*.qcow2) continue ;; esac; `+
199: 			`if [ -n "$src" ]; then src=""; break; fi; `+
200: 			`src="$f"; `+
201: 			`done; `+
202: 			`fi; `+
203: 			`fi; `+
204: 			`[ -n "$src" ] || exit 0; `+
205: 			`ln "$src" "$alias"`,
206: 		aliasName,
207: 	)
208: 
209: 	execCmd := clabexec.NewExecCmdFromSlice([]string{"sh", "-lc", cmd})
210: 	res, err := d.RunExec(ctx, execCmd)
211: 	if err != nil {
212: 		log.Warnf(
213: 			"node %q pre-stop vrnetlab qcow alias preparation failed: %v",
214: 			d.Config().ShortName,
215: 			err,
216: 		)
217: 		return
218: 	}
219: 
220: 	if res != nil && res.ReturnCode != 0 {
221: 		log.Warnf(
222: 			"node %q pre-stop vrnetlab qcow alias prep returned code %d (stderr: %s)",
223: 			d.Config().ShortName,
224: 			res.ReturnCode,
225: 			res.Stderr,
226: 		)
227: 	}
228: }
229: 
230: func vrnetlabQcowAliasName(image string) (string, bool) {
231: 	tag, ok := imageTag(image)
232: 	if !ok {
233: 		return "", false
234: 	}
235: 
236: 	return "clab-" + tag + ".qcow2", true
237: }
238: 
239: func imageTag(image string) (string, bool) {
240: 	if at := strings.LastIndex(image, "@"); at != -1 {
241: 		image = image[:at]
242: 	}
243: 
244: 	lastSlash := strings.LastIndex(image, "/")
245: 	lastColon := strings.LastIndex(image, ":")
246: 	if lastColon == -1 || lastColon < lastSlash {
247: 		return "", false
248: 	}
249: 
250: 	tag := image[lastColon+1:]
251: 	if tag == "" {
252: 		return "", false
253: 	}
254: 
255: 	if !imageTagRE.MatchString(tag) {
256: 		return "", false
257: 	}
258: 
259: 	return tag, true
260: }

Reasoning
So `CalculateInterfaceIndex`: index = parsedIndex - InterfaceOffset + FirstDataIfIndex. With InterfaceOffset=2, FirstDataIfIndex=1: ether2 → 2-2+1 = 1 → eth1. ether1 → 1-2+1 = 0 < 1 → error. ether5 → 5-2+1=4 → eth4. Matches test. And "ethX" names: VRNode.AddEndpoint checks VMInterfaceRegexp `eth[1-9]\d*$` first — "eth1" matches, so passes through unchanged. Good. And "eth0"? `eth[1-9]\d*$` matches "eth1", "eth12" — does it match "eth0"? No, requires first digit 1-9. So eth0 → GetMappedInterfaceName("eth0") → InterfaceRegexp `ether(?P<port>\d+)` doesn't match "eth0" → error. Good.

Wait — but the launcher maps container ethN → RouterOS ether(N+1). And RouterOS ether2 = eth1. Consistent.

Now check `LoadStartupConfigFileVr` to see what it does (copies resolved startup config to LabDir/ConfigDirName/StartupCfgFName).

10: // orchestration-ready (containerlab#3116, #3464).
11: package mikrotik_chr
12: 
13: import (
14: 	"context"
15: 	_ "embed"
16: 	"fmt"
17: 	"os"
18: 	"path"
19: 	"path/filepath"
20: 	"regexp"
21: 	"slices"
22: 	"strings"
23: 	"time"
24: 
25: 	"github.com/charmbracelet/log"
26: 	clabconstants "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/constants"
27: 	clabnodes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/nodes"
28: 	clabtypes "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/types"
29: 	clabutils "github.com/srl-labs/containerlab/utils"
30: 	"golang.org/x/crypto/ssh"
31: )
32: 
33: var (
34: 	kindNames          = []string{"mikrotik_chr"}
35: 	defaultCredentials = clabnodes.NewCredentials("admin", "admin")
36: 
37: 	InterfaceRegexp = regexp.MustCompile(`ether(?P<port>\d+)`)
38: 	InterfaceOffset = 2
39: 	InterfaceHelp   = "etherX (where X >= 2) or ethX (where X >= 1)"
40: 
41: 	//go:embed chr-launch.sh
42: 	launcherScript []byte
43: )
44: 
45: const (
46: 	configDirName   = "ftpboot"
47: 	startupCfgFName = "config.auto.rsc"
48: 
49: 	launcherFName = "chr-launch.sh"
50: 	launcherPath  = "/chr-launch.sh"
51: 	// readyFile is written by the launcher once RouterOS accepted the credentials and the
52: 	// startup config, and removed at every container start.
53: 	readyFile = "/.chr-launch-ready"
54: 
55: 	// mgmtComment tags every management object the launcher creates in RouterOS.
56: 	mgmtComment = "clab-mgmt"
57: 
58: 	scrapliPlatformName = "mikrotik_routeros" //nolint: misspell
59: )
60: 
61: // Register registers the node in the NodeRegistry.
62: func Register(r *clabnodes.NodeRegistry) {
63: 	platformAttrs := &clabnodes.PlatformAttrs{
64: 		ScrapliPlatformName: scrapliPlatformName,
65: 	}
66: 
67: 	nrea := clabnodes.NewNodeRegistryEntryAttributes(defaultCredentials, nil, platformAttrs)
68: 
69: 	r.Register(kindNames, func() clabnodes.Node {
70: 		return new(mikrotikCHR)
71: 	}, nrea)
72: }
73: 
74: type mikrotikCHR struct {
75: 	clabnodes.VRNode
76: }
77: 
78: func (n *mikrotikCHR) Init(cfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig, opts ...clabnodes.NodeOption) error {
79: 	n.VRNode = *clabnodes.NewVRNode(n, defaultCredentials, scrapliPlatformName)
80: 	n.ConfigDirName = configDirName
81: 	n.StartupCfgFName = startupCfgFName
82: 	// The image's qemu falls back to TCG emulation without /dev/kvm: KVM makes the boot
83: 	// fast but is not required.
84: 	n.HostRequirements.VirtRequired = false
85: 
86: 	n.Cfg = cfg
87: 	for _, o := range opts {
88: 		o(n)
89: 	}
90: 
91: 	// Credentials travel as env, not on the command line, so a password needs no quoting.
92: 	defEnv := map[string]string{
93: 		"USERNAME": n.Cfg.Credentials.Username,
94: 		"PASSWORD": n.Cfg.Credentials.Password,
95: 	}
96: 	n.Cfg.Env = clabutils.MergeStringMaps(defEnv, n.Cfg.Env)
97: 
98: 	n.Cfg.Binds = append(n.Cfg.Binds,
99: 		fmt.Sprint(path.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, configDirName), ":/ftpboot"),
100: 		fmt.Sprint(path.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, launcherFName), ":", launcherPath, ":ro"),
101: 	)
102: 
103: 	if n.Cfg.Entrypoint == "" {
104: 		n.Cfg.Entrypoint = "/bin/sh " + launcherPath
105: 	}
106: 	n.Cfg.Cmd = fmt.Sprintf("--hostname %s", n.Cfg.ShortName)
107: 
108: 	if n.Cfg.Healthcheck == nil {
109: 		n.Cfg.Healthcheck = &clabtypes.HealthcheckConfig{
110: 			Test:        []string{"CMD-SHELL", "test -f " + readyFile},
111: 			Interval:    5,
112: 			Timeout:     3,
113: 			Retries:     3,
114: 			StartPeriod: 300,
115: 		}
116: 	}
117: 
118: 	n.InterfaceRegexp = InterfaceRegexp
119: 	n.InterfaceOffset = InterfaceOffset
120: 	n.InterfaceHelp = InterfaceHelp
121: 
122: 	return nil
123: }
124: 
125: // PreDeploy writes the launcher next to the startup config on every deploy, so a
126: // containerlab upgrade ships its launcher to existing labs.
127: func (n *mikrotikCHR) PreDeploy(ctx context.Context, params *clabnodes.PreDeployParams) error {
128: 	if err := n.VRNode.PreDeploy(ctx, params); err != nil {
129: 		return err
130: 	}
131: 
132: 	dst := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, launcherFName)
133: 	if err := os.WriteFile(dst, launcherScript, clabconstants.PermissionsFileDefault); err != nil {
134: 		return fmt.Errorf("node %q: failed to write %s: %w", n.Cfg.ShortName, dst, err)
135: 	}
136: 
137: 	return nil
138: }
139: 
140: // PreStop skips VRNode's vrnetlab qcow2 alias step, which does not apply to this image.
141: func (*mikrotikCHR) PreStop(_ context.Context) error {
142: 	return nil
143: }
144: 
145: // SaveConfig exports the RouterOS configuration over SSH (scrapligo has no RouterOS
146: // platform) and writes it as the node's startup config, without the objects the launcher
147: // creates at every boot.
148: func (n *mikrotikCHR) SaveConfig(_ context.Context) (*clabnodes.SaveConfigResult, error) {
149: 	config := &ssh.ClientConfig{
150: 		User:            n.Cfg.Credentials.Username,
151: 		Auth:            []ssh.AuthMethod{ssh.Password(n.Cfg.Credentials.Password)},
152: 		HostKeyCallback: ssh.InsecureIgnoreHostKey(), // lab node, host key unknown
153: 		Timeout:         10 * time.Second,
154: 	}
155: 
156: 	conn, err := ssh.Dial("tcp", fmt.Sprintf("%s:22", n.Cfg.LongName), config)
157: 	if err != nil {
158: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to connect to %s via SSH: %w", n.Cfg.LongName, err)
159: 	}
160: 	defer conn.Close()
161: 
162: 	session, err := conn.NewSession()
163: 	if err != nil {
164: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create SSH session for %s: %w", n.Cfg.LongName, err)
165: 	}
166: 	defer session.Close()
167: 
168: 	output, err := session.CombinedOutput("/export")
169: 	if err != nil {
170: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to export the configuration of %s: %w", n.Cfg.LongName, err)
171: 	}
172: 
173: 	exported := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
174: 	if exported == "" {
175: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("received empty configuration from %s", n.Cfg.LongName)
176: 	}
177: 
178: 	configPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, n.ConfigDirName, n.StartupCfgFName)
179: 	err = os.WriteFile(configPath, []byte(filterLauncherConfig(exported)+"\n"),
180: 		clabconstants.PermissionsOpen) // skipcq: GO-S2306
181: 	if err != nil {
182: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write config by %s path from %s container: %w",
183: 			configPath, n.Cfg.ShortName, err)
184: 	}
185: 	log.Info("Saved configuration to path", "nodeName", n.Cfg.ShortName, "path", configPath)
186: 
187: 	return &clabnodes.SaveConfigResult{ConfigPath: configPath}, nil
188: }
189: 
190: // filterLauncherConfig drops what the launcher configures at every boot from an /export:
191: // every object tagged clab-mgmt (the ether1 management addresses, the mgmt routing table,
192: // its routes and rules), the ether1 ND entry and CHR's ether1 DHCP client. Imported again
193: // as a startup config, these lines duplicate objects the launcher already created and
194: // RouterOS stops the import at the first one; the management addressing also belongs to
195: // the deployment, not the lab. Continuation lines (an unescaped trailing backslash) are joined
196: // before
197: // matching.
198: func filterLauncherConfig(config string) string {
199: 	var out []string
200: 
201: 	section := ""
202: 	pending := ""
203: 	for line := range strings.SplitSeq(strings.ReplaceAll(config, "\r\n", "\n"), "\n") {
204: 		if pending != "" {
205: 			line = pending + "\n" + line
206: 			pending = ""
207: 		}
208: 		if continues(line) {
209: 			pending = line
210: 			continue
211: 		}
212: 
213: 		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
214: 		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "/") {
215: 			section = trimmed
216: 		}
217: 		if launcherOwned(section, trimmed) {
218: 			continue
219: 		}
220: 		out = append(out, line)
221: 	}
222: 	if pending != "" {
223: 		out = append(out, pending)
224: 	}
225: 
226: 	return strings.Join(out, "\n")
227: }
228: 
229: // continues reports whether an /export line continues on the next one: it ends in an
230: // unescaped backslash. RouterOS writes a literal backslash in a value as `\\`, so an even
231: // run of trailing backslashes ends the line.
232: func continues(line string) bool {
233: 	trailing := len(line) - len(strings.TrimRight(line, `\`))
234: 
235: 	return trailing%2 == 1
236: }
237: 
238: func launcherOwned(section, line string) bool {
239: 	if !strings.HasPrefix(line, "add ") {
240: 		return false
241: 	}
242: 
243: 	fields := strings.Fields(strings.ReplaceAll(line, `\`, " "))
244: 	has := func(kv string) bool { return slices.Contains(fields, kv) }
245: 
246: 	if has("comment=" + mgmtComment) {
247: 		return true
248: 	}
249: 
250: 	// /ipv6 nd keeps no comment: the launcher's ether1 entry is matched by interface.
251: 	switch section {
252: 	case "/ip address", "/ipv6 address", "/ip dhcp-client", "/ipv6 nd":
253: 		return has("interface=ether1")
254: 	}
255: 
256: 	return false
257: }

(End of file - total 257 lines)

Imported again\n// as a startup config, these lines duplicate objects the launcher already created and\n// RouterOS stops the import at the first one; the management addressing also belongs to\n// the deployment, not the lab. 28: 	defaultCredentials = clabnodes.NewCredentials("admin", "admin")
29: 
30: 	InterfaceRegexp = regexp.MustCompile(`ether(?P<port>\d+)`)
31: 	InterfaceOffset = 2
32: 	InterfaceHelp   = "etherX (where X >= 2) or ethX (where X >= 1)"
33: )
34: 
35: const (
36: 	configDirName   = "ftpboot"
37: 	startupCfgFName = "config.auto.rsc"
38:

40: )
41: 
42: // Register registers the node in the NodeRegistry.
43: func Register(r *clabnodes.NodeRegistry) {
44: 	platformAttrs := &clabnodes.PlatformAttrs{
45: 		ScrapliPlatformName: scrapliPlatformName,
46: 	}
47: 
48: 	nrea := clabnodes.NewNodeRegistryEntryAttributes(defaultCredentials, nil, platformAttrs)
49: 
50: 	r.Register(kindNames, func() clabnodes.Node {
51: 		return new(vrRos)
52: 	}, nrea)

55: type vrRos struct {
56: 	clabnodes.VRNode
57: }
58: 
59: func (n *vrRos) Init(cfg *clabtypes.NodeConfig, opts ...clabnodes.NodeOption) error {
60: 	// Init VRNode
61: 	n.VRNode = *clabnodes.NewVRNode(n, defaultCredentials, scrapliPlatformName)
62: 	n.ConfigDirName = configDirName
63: 	n.StartupCfgFName = startupCfgFName
64: 	// set virtualization requirement
65: 	n.HostRequirements.VirtRequired = true
66: 
67: 	n.Cfg = cfg
68: 	for _, o := range opts {
69: 		o(n)
70: 	}
71: 	defEnv := map[string]string{
72: 		"CONNECTION_MODE":    clabnodes.VrDefConnMode,
73: 		"USERNAME":           n.Cfg.Credentials.Username,
74: 		"PASSWORD":           n.Cfg.Credentials.Password,
75: 		"DOCKER_NET_V4_ADDR": n.Mgmt.IPv4Subnet,
76: 		"DOCKER_NET_V6_ADDR": n.Mgmt.IPv6Subnet,
77: 	}
78: 	n.Cfg.Env = clabutils.MergeStringMaps(defEnv, n.Cfg.Env)
79: 
80: 	n.Cfg.Binds = append(n.Cfg.Binds, fmt.Sprint(path.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, "ftpboot"), ":/ftpboot"))
81: 
82: 	if n.Cfg.Env["CONNECTION_MODE"] == "macvtap" {
83: 		// mount dev dir to enable macvtap
84: 		n.Cfg.Binds = append(n.Cfg.Binds, "/dev:/dev")
85: 	}
86: 
87: 	n.Cfg.Cmd = fmt.Sprintf(
88: 		"--username %s --password %s --hostname %s --connection-mode %s --trace",
89: 		n.Cfg.Env["USERNAME"],
90: 		n.Cfg.Env["PASSWORD"],
91: 		n.Cfg.ShortName,
92: 		n.Cfg.Env["CONNECTION_MODE"],
93: 	)
94: 
95: 	n.InterfaceRegexp = InterfaceRegexp
96: 	n.InterfaceOffset = InterfaceOffset
97: 	n.InterfaceHelp = InterfaceHelp
98: 
99: 	return nil
100: }
101: 
102: // SaveConfig overrides the default VRNode SaveConfig to handle MikroTik RouterOS
103: // Uses direct SSH connection since scrapligo doesn't support MikroTik RouterOS platform.
104: // To be refactored to use scrapli's GenericDriver or an enhanced scraplicfg.
105: func (n *vrRos) SaveConfig(_ context.Context) (*clabnodes.SaveConfigResult, error) {
106: 	// Create SSH client configuration
107: 	config := &ssh.ClientConfig{
108: 		User: n.Cfg.Credentials.Username,
109: 		Auth: []ssh.AuthMethod{
110: 			ssh.Password(n.Cfg.Credentials.Password),
111: 		},
112: 		HostKeyCallback: ssh.InsecureIgnoreHostKey(), // Accept any host key
113: 		Timeout:         10 * time.Second,
114: 	}
115: 
116: 	// Connect to the MikroTik RouterOS device
117: 	conn, err := ssh.Dial("tcp", fmt.Sprintf("%s:22", n.Cfg.LongName), config)
118: 	if err != nil {
119: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to connect to %s via SSH: %+v", n.Cfg.LongName, err)
120: 	}
121: 	defer conn.Close()
122: 
123: 	// Create a session
124: 	session, err := conn.NewSession()
125: 	if err != nil {
126: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create SSH session for %s: %+v", n.Cfg.LongName, err)
127: 	}
128: 	defer session.Close()
129: 
130: 	// Execute the export command to get the configuration
131: 	output, err := session.CombinedOutput("export")
132: 	if err != nil {
133: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to execute export command on %s: %+v", n.Cfg.LongName, err)
134: 	}
135: 
136: 	config_content := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
137: 	if config_content == "" {
138: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("received empty configuration from %s", n.Cfg.LongName)
139: 	}
140: 
141: 	// Filter out ether1 management interface IP address configuration
142: 	filtered_config := n.filterManagementInterfaceConfig(config_content)
143: 
144: 	// Save config to mounted labdir startup config path
145: 	configPath := filepath.Join(n.Cfg.LabDir, n.ConfigDirName, n.StartupCfgFName)
146: 	err = os.WriteFile(configPath, []byte(filtered_config),
147: 		clabconstants.PermissionsOpen) // skipcq: GO-S2306
148: 	if err != nil {
149: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf(
150: 			"failed to write config by %s path from %s container: %v",
151: 			configPath,
152: 			n.Cfg.ShortName,
153: 			err,
154: 		)
155: 	}
156: 	log.Info("Saved configuration to path", "nodeName", n.Cfg.ShortName, "path", configPath)
157: 
158: 	return &clabnodes.SaveConfigResult{
159: 		ConfigPath: configPath,
160: 	}, nil
161: }
162: 
163: // filterManagementInterfaceConfig removes ether1 (management interface) IP address configuration
164: // from the exported RouterOS configuration to avoid including containerlab management IP settings.
165: func (n *vrRos) filterManagementInterfaceConfig(config string) string {
166: 	lines := strings.Split(config, "\n")
167: 	filteredLines := make([]string, 0, len(lines))
168: 	inIPAddrSection := false
169: 
170: 	for _, line := range lines {
171: 		trimmedLine := strings.TrimSpace(line)
172: 		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmedLine, "/") {
173: 			inIPAddrSection = trimmedLine == "/ip address"
174: 		}
175: 
176: 		if inIPAddrSection &&
177: 			strings.HasPrefix(trimmedLine, "add address=") &&
178: 			slices.Contains(strings.Fields(trimmedLine), "interface=ether1") {
179: 			continue
180: 		}
181: 
182: 		filteredLines = append(filteredLines, line)
183: 	}
184: 
185: 	return strings.Join(filteredLines, "\n")
186: }

Actually opening a FIFO for writing blocks until a reader opens it — nc opened reader side first (nc runs in background with <"$_f"), so open proceeds. Then if nc exits while writer still open, writes get SIGPIPE. busybox sh may not ignore SIGPIPE → script dies with SIGPIPE. That's a potential bug: `printf ... >&7` after nc has exited → SIGPIPE kills the script (default termination). The wrapper `timeout ... nc` handles the timeout of nc, but if nc exits early (connection refused), and then the while loop: `kill -0 "$_pid"` fails → loop exits immediately → `exec 7>&-` closes fd → no more writes. Actually the printf happens BEFORE the loop: sequence is: start nc bg, open fifo fd7, printf request. If nc already exited by then (connection refused immediately), printf could get EPIPE/SIGPIPE.
